 Definitions
 釋義



 “AUD”                          Australian dollar, the lawful currency of Australia
「澳元」                            澳大利亞元，澳大利亞法定貨幣

 “Audit Committee”              the audit committee of the Board
「審核委員會」                         董事會審核委員會

 “Board”                        the board of Directors of our Company
「董事會」                           本公司董事會

 “China” or “PRC”               the People’s Republic of China, which, for the purpose of this report and for
                                geographic reference only, excludes Hong Kong, the Macao Special
                                Administrative Region of the PRC and Taiwan
「中國」                            中華人民共和國，僅就本報告及地區參考而言，不包括香港、中國澳門特別
                                行政區及台灣

 “Company” or “our Company”     Greentown Service Group Co. Ltd. ( 綠 城 服 務 集 團 有 限 公 司 ), an exempted
                                company incorporated in the Cayman Islands with limited liability on 24
                                November 2014
「公司」或「本公司」                      綠城服務集團有限公司，一家於 2014 年 11 月 24 日於開曼群島註冊成立的獲
                                豁免有限公司

 “Director(s)”                  the director(s) of our Company
「董事」                            本公司董事

 “GFA”                          gross floor area
「建築面積」                          總建築面積

 “Group”, “we”, “us” or “our”   our Company and its subsidiary(ies) from time to time or where the context so
                                requires, in respect of the period before our Company became the holding
                                company of our present subsidiaries, the business operated by such
                                subsidiaries or their predecessors (as the case may be)
「集團」、「本集團」或「我們」                 本公司及其不時的附屬公司，而倘文義所需，就本公司成為現有附屬公司的
                                控股公司前的期間，指該等附屬公司或其前身公司（視乎情況而定）經營的業
                                務

 “HK$”                          Hong Kong dollar, the lawful currency of Hong Kong
「港元」                            港元，香港法定貨幣

 “Hong Kong”                    the Hong Kong Special Administrative Region of the PRC
「香港」                            中國香港特別行政區

                                this report for the purpose of ascertaining certain information contained in this
                                report
「最後實際可行日期」                      2026 年 9 月 11 日，即本報告付印前為確定當中所載若干資料之最後實際可行
                                日期

Company Overview
公司概覽



The Group is a leading happy living service provider nationwide, and is    本集團是中國具領先地位的幸福生活服務商，
based on people’s full range of life scenes and the entire life cycle of   圍繞人的全生活場景及房地產的全生命週期，
real estate, and provides a wide range of services that are strongly       提 供 多 元 化、與 生 活 呈 現 強 關 聯 的 服 務，包
associated with life, which include three types of services, namely        括物業服務、園區服務及諮詢服務三類業務，
property services, community living services and consulting services,      為住戶提供種類廣泛的產品及服務組合，滿
and provide our residents with access to a broad portfolio of products     足客戶、業主及住戶不斷變化的日常生活需求。
and services that address the evolving daily needs of our customers,
property owners and residents.

OUR PRODUCT AND SERVICE PORTFOLIO                                          我們的產品及服務組合
•    Property services. We provide a range of high quality property        •   物業服務。我們提供一系列高品質的物
     management services, such as security, cleaning, gardening, and           業管理服務，例如我們統稱為「標準」物
     property repair and maintenance services, which we refer to               業管理服務的保安、保潔、園藝、物業
     collectively as “standard” property management services, and              維修及保養服務，並就該等服務向在管
     charge the residents and property owners of our managed                   物業的住戶及業主或房地產開發商收取
     properties or real estate developers management fees for such             服 務 費。於 2026 年 6 月 30 日，我 們 的 總
     services. As at 30 June 2026, we managed properties located in            在 管 合 同 建 築 面 積 達 580.3 百 萬 平 方 米
     194 cities across 31 provinces, direct-controlled municipalities          的物業，涵蓋居民高端住宅、政府基礎
     and autonomous regions across China with a total contracted               建設、商業寫字樓、城市綜合體等，覆
     GFA under management of 580.3 million sq.m. which cover                   蓋 全 中 國 31 個 省、直 轄 市 及 自 治 區 的
     properties such as high-end residential buildings, government             194 個城市。
     infrastructure, commercial office buildings and urban complexes.

•    Community living services. Based on the needs of the property         •   園區服務。我們基於業主的全生活場景
     owners’ full range of life scenes, we provide property owners and         的需求，透過多種渠道向我們在管園區
     residents of our managed communities with access to an                    的業主及住戶提供各類日常生活必需品
     extensive array of daily necessities complemented by a wide               和各種品質生活產品和服務。園區服務
     assortment of life-style products and services through a variety of       包括園區產品及服務、家居生活服務、
     channels. Our community living services include community                 園區空間服務、物業資產管理服務、文
     products and services, home living services, community space              化教育服務。隨著業主和住客為更好的
     services, property asset management services, cultural and                生活而衍生的生活需要，集團在創新和
     education services. As the needs of our property owners and               提升傳統優勢服務的基礎上，將繼續運
     residents for better living have been evolving, on the basis of           用移動互聯網與智慧社區入口，部署提
     innovating and upgrading traditional superior services, the Group         供種類更廣的服務和產品來滿足客戶，
     will continue to use the mobile Internet and the smart community          用線上線下結合的方式，拓寬和加速生

businesses”. At the time of investment or equity cooperation, the              行投資或股權合作時，會重點考慮標的公司
Company focuses on factors such as the extent to which the target              與本集團業務的契合度、是否能夠提升本集
company’s business fits the Group’s business, whether the target               團於重點佈局區域的市場份額、標的公司對
company can increase the Group’s market shares in key regions, the             本集團規模的貢獻程度，以及合作方後續可
extent to which the target company contributes to the scale of the             能帶來的資源導入等因素；同時關注標的公
Group, and resources that the partner may bring in the future and              司對生活服務輸出的需求度、能否提升本集
other factors. At the same time, the Company pays attention to details         團生活服務的覆蓋密度以及有關收購對價等
such as the target company’s demand for living service output, whether         內容。
it can improve the coverage density of living service of the Group, and
the consideration of the relevant acquisition.

on the main businesses, and at the same time strictly implement the            業務，同時嚴格落實投資原則，包括：(i) 於投
investment principles, including: (i) in terms of investment strategy, we      資 策 略 上，聚 焦 本 集 團 主 營 業 務，加 強 被 收
will focus on the Group’s main businesses, strengthen the management           購標的公司的經營效益管理，加快虧損項目
of the operational efficiency of the target company acquired, and              的 止 損 或 處 置；(ii) 於 投 資 方 向 上，以 本 集 團

of investment direction, we will be guided by the Group’s development          同效應；(iii) 於投資領域上，以本公司現有業

and regions that are complementary with the Company; and (iv) in

order to bring a better contribution to the development of the Group.

     monitor its exposure to exchange rate and interest rate risks and           大銀行探討外匯對沖方案，於有需要時利用

     persons responsible for financial and accounting matters, and applying     閱的範圍遠較按照香港審計準則進行審核的
     analytical and other review procedures. A review is substantially less     範圍為小，所以不能保證我們會注意到在審
     in scope than an audit conducted in accordance with Hong Kong              核中可能會被發現的所有重大事項。因此我
     Standards on Auditing and consequently does not enable us to obtain        們不會發表任何審核意見。
     assurance that we would become aware of all significant matters that
     might be identified in an audit. Accordingly we do not express an audit
     opinion.

           is applied, since Greentown Service Group (Hong Kong) Co.                   務 集 團（香 港）有 限 公 司（「綠 城 服

           Region under the “Arrangement between the Mainland of                       證為香港特別行政區稅務居民企

               price on the grant date and the average closing price for                於授出日期的收市價與緊接授出
               the five business days immediately before the grant date                 日期前五個營業日的平均收市價
               was HKD4.328. The options have vesting periods of 3 years                中 的 較 高 者 為 4.328 港 元。該 等 購

22 POSSIBLE IMPACT OF AMENDMENTS, NEW                                   22 截 至 2026 年 6 月 30 日 止 六 個
   STANDARDS, AND INTERPRETATIONS                                          月已頒佈但尚未生效的修訂、
   ISSUED BUT NOT YET EFFECTIVE FOR THE                                    新準則及詮釋可能產生的影
   SIX MONTHS ENDED 30 JUNE 2026                                           響
    A number of amendments and new standards are not yet                   若 干 修 訂 及 新 訂 準 則 對 自 2026 年 1 月 1
    mandatory for annual periods beginning 1 January 2026. Earlier         日起開始之年度期間尚未強制生效，允
    application is permitted; however, the Group has not early             許提早採納；然而，本集團於編製本中
    adopted any new or amended standards in preparing this interim         期財務報告時並無提早採納任何新訂或
    financial report.                                                      經修訂準則。

    The following updates the information provided in the last annual      以下內容對上一份年度財務報表所提供
    financial statements about the possible impacts of HKFRS 18            有關香港財務報告準則第 18 號可能產生
    which may have a significant impact on the Group’s consolidated        的影響資料進行更新，該準則於採納時
    financial statements when adopted.                                     可能對本集團綜合財務報表產生重大影
                                                                           響。

   HKFRS 18, Presentation and disclosure in financial                      香港財務報告準則第 18 號，財務報
   statements                                                              表之列報及披露
   HKFRS 18 will replace HKAS 1, Presentation of financial                 香港財務報告準則第 18 號將取代香港會
   statements , and is first effective for annual reporting periods        計準則第 1 號財務報表的呈列 ，並將首
   beginning on or after 1 January 2027.                                   次 於 2027 年 1 月 1 日 或 之 後 開 始 的 年 度
                                                                           報告期間生效。

    HKFRS 18 introduces new requirements for presentation and              香港財務報告準則第 18 號引入財務報表
    disclosure in financial statements, including new categories and       呈列及披露的新規定，包括損益表內的
    defined subtotals in the statement of profit or loss, enhanced         新類別及已定義小計、加強資料匯總及
    requirements for aggregation and disaggregation of information,        分拆的規定，以及有關管理層界定績效
    and specific disclosures about management-defined performance          指標的特定披露。本集團現正評估首次
    measures. The Group is assessing the expected impact that              應用香港財務報告準則第 18 號對其綜合
    the initial application of HKFRS 18 will have on its consolidated      財務報表的預期影響。
    financial statements.

    The Group expects that the adoption of HKFRS 18 will not               本集團預期採納香港財務報告準則第 18
    change the Group’s net assets or net profit. However, it is            號將不會改變本集團的資產淨值或淨利
    expected to affect the presentation of the Group’s consolidated        潤。然而，預期其將影響本集團綜合損
    statement of profit or loss and related note disclosures. Based        益表的呈列及相關附註披露。根據迄今
    on the work performed to date, the Group’s assessment on               已進行的工作，本集團對首次應用香港
    the expected impact of the initial application of HKFRS 18 is          財務報告準則第 18 號的預期影響之評估
    summarised below. The assessment remains subject to change as          概述如下。隨著本集團持續推進實施工
    the Group continues its implementation work.                           作，該評估仍可能有所變動。

      22 POSSIBLE IMPACT OF AMENDMENTS, NEW                                      22 截 至 2026 年 6 月 30 日 止 六 個
         STANDARDS, AND INTERPRETATIONS                                             月已頒佈但尚未生效的修訂、
         ISSUED BUT NOT YET EFFECTIVE FOR THE                                       新準則及詮釋可能產生的影
         SIX MONTHS ENDED 30 JUNE 2026 (Continued)                                  響（續）
          (a)   Structure of the statement of profit or loss                        (a)   損益表的結構
                HKFRS 18 requires entities to classify income and expenses                香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第 18 號 規 定 實
                into specified categories in the statement of profit or loss,             體須將收入及開支於損益表內分
                including operating, investing, and financing categories.                 類 為 指 定 類 別，包 括 經 營、投 資
